# Agents · A · part 8

1,125 entries, ag-innovate-agent to agent-architect. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **ag-innovate-agent**: INNOVATE MODE - Brainstorming and option exploration. Use for discussing implementation approaches, architectural trade-offs, and technical designs. Never writes plan files or code. ([ngxccc/agent-skills-kit](https://github.com/ngxccc/agent-skills-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ag-innovate-agent.md))
- **ag-market-analyst**: Use this agent for the market view — commodity price, input cost trends, marketing/hedging frames, and basis. NOT for agronomy (route to crop-agronomist) or per-acre economics (route to farm-operations-analyst). ([mcorbett51090/RavenClaude](https://github.com/mcorbett51090/RavenClaude/blob/HEAD/plugins/precision-agriculture/agents/ag-market-analyst.md) · ★9)
- **ag-plan-agent**: PLAN MODE - Creating exhaustive technical specifications and implementation plans. Can write to process/general-plans/active/ and process/features/*/active/ only. Use after approach is decided. ([ngxccc/agent-skills-kit](https://github.com/ngxccc/agent-skills-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ag-plan-agent.md))
- **ag-planner**: Planning mode, architecture ([Artawower/.config](https://github.com/Artawower/.config/blob/HEAD/.pi/agent/agents/ag-planner.md) · ★2)
- **ag-planner**: Fresh-context architect for Agentile. Reads a Ready spec plus the standing context (CLAUDE.md, ADRs) and returns an implementation plan — files to touch, approach, test strategy, risks — without editing any code. Use during /ag-plan for sizable or risky specs, or whenever you want an independent read of the approach before building. ([agenta-consulting/agentile](https://github.com/agenta-consulting/agentile/blob/HEAD/agents/ag-planner.md))
- **ag-quick-fix-agent**: Quick-fix lane for small low-risk changes. Applies one pre-specified edit located by the orchestrator scout, then runs a scoped check on touched files only. No plan file, no validate-contract, no EVL, no UPDATE PROCESS. Aborts to full RESEARCH if the change touches schema, auth, API, billing, or migration surfaces or grows beyond a small bounded scope. ([ngxccc/agent-skills-kit](https://github.com/ngxccc/agent-skills-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ag-quick-fix-agent.md))
- **ag-research-agent**: RESEARCH MODE - Information gathering only. Use for understanding existing code, architecture, and context. Never suggests implementations or modifications. ([ngxccc/agent-skills-kit](https://github.com/ngxccc/agent-skills-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ag-research-agent.md))
- **ag-researcher**: Research mode, gathers info online, explores project ([Artawower/.config](https://github.com/Artawower/.config/blob/HEAD/.pi/agent/agents/ag-researcher.md) · ★2)
- **ag-reviewer**: Code review agent ([Artawower/.config](https://github.com/Artawower/.config/blob/HEAD/.pi/agent/agents/ag-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **ag-reviewer**: Fresh-context reviewer for Agentile — the "trust but verify" gate. Critiques the builder's diff against the spec, runs the project's gates, and does a security skim, with no stake in the implementation. Use after ag-builder finishes and before merge. Agents catch others' mistakes better than their own, so this must be a separate context from the builder. ([agenta-consulting/agentile](https://github.com/agenta-consulting/agentile/blob/HEAD/agents/ag-reviewer.md))
- **ag-spec-agent**: SPEC MODE - Product-discovery requirements doc for user review. Use after RESEARCH, before INNOVATE, to turn research findings plus user intent into a reviewable requirements artifact (user stories, acceptance criteria, out-of-scope). Never chooses an approach or writes implementation steps. ([ngxccc/agent-skills-kit](https://github.com/ngxccc/agent-skills-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ag-spec-agent.md))
- **ag-tester**: Validate code quality through testing: unit/integration tests, test coverage analysis, error handling, performance requirements, and build verification. Call after implementing features or significant code changes. ([ngxccc/agent-skills-kit](https://github.com/ngxccc/agent-skills-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ag-tester.md))
- **ag-ui-ux-designer**: UI/UX execution support including interface implementation, design-system polish, responsive layouts, micro-animations, accessibility review, or design documentation. ([ngxccc/agent-skills-kit](https://github.com/ngxccc/agent-skills-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ag-ui-ux-designer.md))
- **ag-update-process-agent**: UPDATE PROCESS MODE - Documenting learnings, updating rules, archiving completed plans, and maintaining repo context. Use after feature implementation is complete. ([ngxccc/agent-skills-kit](https://github.com/ngxccc/agent-skills-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ag-update-process-agent.md))
- **ag-validate-agent**: VALIDATE MODE - Two-layer feasibility and validation fan-out to verify implementation plans before execution. Writes ([ngxccc/agent-skills-kit](https://github.com/ngxccc/agent-skills-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ag-validate-agent.md))
- **ag2-architect**: AG2 architecture advisor that recommends agent patterns and orchestration strategies. Invoke when designing a multi-agent system, choosing between group chat, pipeline, swarm, or nested chat patterns. ([davepoon/buildwithclaude](https://github.com/davepoon/buildwithclaude/blob/HEAD/plugins/ag2-agent-builder/agents/ag2-architect.md) · ★3.8k · 5 copies)
- **ag2-architect**: AG2 architecture advisor that helps choose the right agent patterns, orchestration strategies, and design approaches for multi-agent systems. Consult before building to get the design right. ([ag2ai/ag2-claude-plugins](https://github.com/ag2ai/ag2-claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/ag2-agent-guide/agents/ag2-architect.md) · ★5)
- **ag2-architect**: Expert AG2 framework architect that helps design multi-agent systems, select the right patterns, and avoid common pitfalls. Use for planning and design decisions before writing code. ([lzy18001500226/MoSim](https://github.com/lzy18001500226/MoSim/blob/HEAD/References/Agent/Frameworks/ag2/cli/src/ag2_cli/content/skills/agents/ag2-architect.md))
- **ag2-migrator**: Helps migrate code from old AutoGen 0.2 patterns to the current AG2 API. Use when working with legacy AutoGen code that needs updating. ([lzy18001500226/MoSim](https://github.com/lzy18001500226/MoSim/blob/HEAD/References/Agent/Frameworks/ag2/cli/src/ag2_cli/content/skills/agents/ag2-migrator.md))
- **ag2-prompt-engineer**: Crafts and reviews system prompts for AG2 agents. Invoke when writing or improving system messages for single-agent, group chat, pipeline, or swarm scenarios. ([davepoon/buildwithclaude](https://github.com/davepoon/buildwithclaude/blob/HEAD/plugins/ag2-agent-builder/agents/ag2-prompt-engineer.md) · ★3.8k · 5 copies)
- **ag2-prompt-engineer**: Helps craft effective system prompts for AG2 agents. Analyzes the agent's role, tools, and orchestration context to produce high-quality system messages that maximize agent reliability. ([ag2ai/ag2-claude-plugins](https://github.com/ag2ai/ag2-claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/ag2-agent-guide/agents/ag2-prompt-engineer.md) · ★5)
- **ag2-reviewer**: Reviews AG2 agent code for tool contract violations, prompt quality, security issues, and best practices. Invoke after creating or modifying AG2 agents. ([davepoon/buildwithclaude](https://github.com/davepoon/buildwithclaude/blob/HEAD/plugins/ag2-agent-builder/agents/ag2-reviewer.md) · ★3.8k · 5 copies)
- **ag2-reviewer**: Reviews AG2 agent implementations for correctness, prompt quality, tool contracts, security, and best practices. Use after creating or modifying AG2 agents. ([ag2ai/ag2-claude-plugins](https://github.com/ag2ai/ag2-claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/ag2-agent-reviewer/agents/ag2-reviewer.md) · ★5)
- **agami-enricher**: Describes ONE chunk of an agami semantic model during onboarding (up to 12 tables of one subject area): table and column descriptions from the build's column profile and the person's documents, entities, and metric ideas, returned as data in files. Launched by agami-connect, one per chunk, in parallel, on a model of more than 12 tables. Read-only: never writes the model, never runs a command, never asks the person anything. ([AgamiAI/agami-core](https://github.com/AgamiAI/agami-core/blob/HEAD/plugins/agami/agents/agami-enricher.md) · ★30)
- **agami-example-writer**: Drafts example questions with their SQL for ONE subject area of an agami semantic model during onboarding, from that area's model bundle (tables, columns, metrics, entities, joins). Launched by agami-connect, one per subject area, in parallel, on a model of more than 12 tables. Read-only: never writes the model, never runs a command, never asks the person anything. ([AgamiAI/agami-core](https://github.com/AgamiAI/agami-core/blob/HEAD/plugins/agami/agents/agami-example-writer.md) · ★30)
- **agatha**: Agatha (Агата) — deep Fable reviewer with an architect's eye: hidden coupling, architectural flaws, subtle edge cases; 60-180 s. Complement to Arthur (Codex) and Dash (fast Sonnet). Use for security-critical, architectural or subtle-bug changes. Triggers: "Агата", "agatha", "глибоке рев'ю", "deep review". ([kaisa-kucherenko/claude-code-flow](https://github.com/kaisa-kucherenko/claude-code-flow/blob/HEAD/agents/agatha.md) · ★25)

- **age-cypher-author**: Especialista Apache AGE + Cypher. Diseña queries de mutación (MERGE/CREATE) e ingesta a `CREATE GRAPH magister` siguiendo el schema dirigido. Crea vistas materializadas por lente. Usalo en Fase 4 o cuando una capa necesite escribir/leer del grafo. ([devwspito/pipeline-cerebro-magister](https://github.com/devwspito/pipeline-cerebro-magister/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/age-cypher-author.md))
- **age-spe-architecture-designer**: Analyzes a discovered process definition and designs the optimal entity architecture by selecting entity types from the decision tree, mapping existing skills, and generating the blueprint with a Mermaid diagram. Use at Step 2 after the S1 handoff JSON is validated and ready for architectural translation. ([luisdomarco/AiAgentArchitect](https://github.com/luisdomarco/AiAgentArchitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/age-spe-architecture-designer.md) · ★1)
- **age-spe-entity-builder**: Generates individual instruction files for each entity in the architectural blueprint, following exact format specifications per entity type and intricacy level, validating each with the user before continuing. Use at Step 3 after the S2 handoff JSON is approved to materialize the designed architecture as deployable files. ([luisdomarco/AiAgentArchitect](https://github.com/luisdomarco/AiAgentArchitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/age-spe-entity-builder.md) · ★1)
- **age-spe-input-enricher**: Receives the user's raw or partial input, analyzes it for completeness, structures it into a validated format, and proposes enrichments to fill detected gaps. Use at Step 0 before Discovery begins, whenever the initial user input is ambiguous, incomplete, or in free-form natural language. ([luisdomarco/AiAgentArchitect](https://github.com/luisdomarco/AiAgentArchitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/age-spe-input-enricher.md) · ★1)
- **age-spe-process-discovery**: Interviews the user to fully discover and document a process or entity to be agentized, applying BPM/BPA techniques, structured questioning, and inverse engineering. Use at Step 1 to transform enriched input into a validated process definition with handoff JSON and AS-IS diagram (Architect mode). ([luisdomarco/AiAgentArchitect](https://github.com/luisdomarco/AiAgentArchitect/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/age-spe-process-discovery.md) · ★1)
- **agencia-desarrollo**: ﻿--- name: agencia-desarrollo description: Coordina la Agencia Desarrollo. Use when la misión requiera transformar contexto funcional o técnico en un workflow de implementación y delegar a Diseño, Desarrollo y QA. tools: - execute - read - edit - search - agent - web - todo - github/* - playwright/* - skill category: agency role: agency_coordinator activation: mode: on_demand use_when: - Usar para ([jguaquetNTT/agencia-transicion](https://github.com/jguaquetNTT/agencia-transicion/blob/HEAD/catalog/desarrollo/agents/agencia-desarrollo.agent.md))
- **agencia-transicion**: Coordina la Agencia Transición.
